# Independent numerical verification report

Verdicts are append-only, newest last. CONFIRMED means precisely the claim stated in that entry, with its stated scope. A confirmed floating calculation is not a rigorous whole-plane norm certificate. OPEN identifies a missing derivation, input, or error bound. REFUTED includes a reproducible contradiction.

No completed checks yet.

## 2026-10-28T20:34:55+00:00: V001, latest archived state and exact reconstruction

Claim: state 131 is the latest retained accepted case4 checkpoint, with the canonical coefficients and center parameters used by analysis2.

Method: inspected the published case4 README, checkpoint inventory, and checkpoint JSON/NPZ; independently expanded the shifted Legendre basis using its three-term polynomial recurrence in rational arithmetic. Recomputed the eliminated center coefficient.

Result: canonical SHA-256 `4d80cf85ea1746d41c3fcfbdc622c74465cff5b9358e68614e521531ea78fb01` agrees exactly. All 860 coefficients agree with the earlier exact reconstruction. c=4076476460239181/2251799813685248, displayed as 1.810319210199998; a0=10.300294891831177. Center strain is exactly 1-c. NPZ P/high inputs match all saved hex numbers, and the NPZ hash matches its checkpoint. State 88 was the old report's independent data cut; state 131 is the later accepted snapshot.

Verdict: **CONFIRMED**, for archive identity and exact reconstruction. No fresh objective integral is claimed here. Evidence: `audit/numerical_audit/research_code/baseline/verification/reconstruction.json`; command: `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 taskset -c 57 nice -n 19 python3 -B audit/numerical_audit/research_code/baseline/code/reconstruct.py`.

## 2026-10-28T20:34:55+00:00: V002, state-131 far-field defects

Claim: the unnormalized state-131 residual grows at infinity and is not in the old unweighted forcing space.

Method: exact rational binomial expansion, independent Cartesian differentiation of homogeneous Fourier sectors, and product-to-sum multiplication. Recomputed every coefficient of E1, E2, E3 and compared exact rationals with the retained earlier audit.

Result: all first-three-sector coefficients agree exactly. The leading exponent is 427123167131315/4076476460239181 = 0.10477753797853507136..., and the full-circle angular norm is enclosed by 0.182078891071125782784877760665237728423348983 with radius below 2e-46. Its sin(39 theta) coefficient is exactly nonzero and displays as -7.698363346447813e-7. Full-circle E2/E3 norms are 4.252816503438653... and 33.29251679648341..., respectively. Quadrant norms are half these values. Seven nonzero sectors at exponent >= -2 remain in the archived four-branch reference.

Verdict: **CONFIRMED**. The residual fails both L2 and homogeneous H^-1 membership, hence cannot satisfy N_s smallness. The argument and its exact scope are in `audit record sector_audit, proof`. Evidence: `audit/numerical_audit/research_code/sector_audit/verification/state131_sectors.json`; command: same launcher with `audit/numerical_audit/research_code/sector_audit/code/sectors.py`.

## 2026-10-28T20:40:22+00:00: N4-003, principal stationary branch coefficients

## 2026-10-28T20:40:22+00:00: N4-005, full-axis normalized objective

Target: the stored C1-BASE-S131 objective 1.2673066866466526e-11 and its exact interpretation, checkpoint SHA-256 `7ff9415cbbe66f9d4a105aa4a999704e67157d8376c969b7561a1c01b1de57bc`.

Method: independent rational differential algebra, exact angular Fourier orthogonality, and radial Beta integrals. The only transcendental terms are finite-part digamma values enclosed by Arb at 768 bits. The exact center identity cancels the origin poles before integration. Derivation: `audit record radial_fourier, proof`. This is independent of the archived training and integration code.

Result: full radial axis J_abs lies in the ball `[1.267306686646650615013976672021911853088527285436244615700150842125407942766284675777448700885423241e-11 +/- 3.73e-113]`. The fixed amplitude is exactly 6741510965989955/281474976710656, approximately 23.95065822465609, not the candidate's current center slope. R_f=F/y; J_abs=(pi a_ref^2)^(-1) integral R_f^2 dtheta d(log r) on the first quadrant. The archived floating objective differs at about 1.6e-15 relative.

Verdict: **CONFIRMED**, VALIDATED for the normalized objective only. This value is not NORM-A2-E2 or NORM-A2-Ns18, which are infinite for C1-BASE-S131. Evidence: `audit/numerical_audit/research_code/radial_fourier/verification/state131_J_768.json`, SHA-256 `cb28f0a45083166f712a4bf445721091059041c195676484f8b0cc39ee1dc475`. ## N4-020. Actual full-plane homogeneous negative norm of d2

- Date: 2026-10-28T21:22:39+00:00.
- Claim: the exact d2 residual has the finite homogeneous H^-1 norm reported below, in the full-plane spatial-Lebesgue normalization.
- Input: canonical profile SHA-256 ff1cb263a566ccf9cbe9321ae42d521ab8672dbb669d191da99d49756d43ef8f; exact D0 polynomial SHA-256 11739d5ef8a6194b5f2e9578749725062cd46b44f535fadf621b6e5d7e99f07e.
- Method: T4-audited finite Green formula, with exact rational endpoint and origin factorization, then Arb2048 polynomial products and positive-denominator integration. ## N4-067. Missing outer-outer factor in the N1 floating negative norm

- Date: 2026-10-28T23:12:01+00:00.
- Claim audited: the frozen N1 ipm_hminus1.py evaluates the full outer-outer Green contribution with its current prefactor.
- Method: read the code and derive the triangle integral independently. The square has two equal geometric triangles, even when the coefficient expansion uses ordered sector pairs. For a,b<-2, I(a,b)=R^(a+b+4)/[(-a-b-4)(n-a-2)]. An explicit source F=r^-4 sin(theta) outside r1 has norm squared pi/12 from both the Green integral and an independently solved piecewise potential. The frozen code would give pi/24.
- Result: the outer prefactor must be pi/n, not pi/(2n). Correcting only this factor in the displayed d2 v4 contributions gives H=390.2169176712514728691548, just0.0000827621289496236 above N4's full-plane value. That remaining discrepancy is not certified or assigned to a cause. The simplified integral also removes a spurious division by zero at b+n+2=0. Runtime 0.001650s.
- Artifacts: audit record negative_norm_tail_audit, proof; frozen N1 code SHA-256 1c227353f2a5316154a17157f8854fad36b39aee5accd755482ee02c6ec009ee; audit/numerical_audit/research_code/negative_norm_tail_audit/verification/factor_audit.json, SHA-256 e8e331274ef29684562fa4b55d3347070892c076dec0889b7c113fb2eef8d3a4. Command: python3 -B audit/numerical_audit/research_code/negative_norm_tail_audit/code/check.py. N1 was notified through this lane's team notes; no file in its lane was changed or executed.
- Verdict: REFUTED for the frozen implementation's full outer-outer normalization. This is an algebraic implementation error with an exact synthetic counterexample. Repair by the owner and validation of remaining quadrature and asymptotic error are separate.

## N4-137. Rigorous scalar moments for several radial scales

- Date: 2026-10-29T02:17:02.141545+00:00; calibration completed 2026-10-29T02:14:02.589336+00:00.
- Claim: for rational b > -1, a rational polynomial P, rational a_i >= 0 and integer m_i >= 1, the integral of v^b P(v) times product_i (1+a_i v)^(-m_i) over [0,1] admits arbitrarily accurate outward enclosures by exact finite-panel binomial approximants.
- Method: use rational panels with relative expansion radius at most 1/3. Bound every omitted binomial tail by a geometric series, telescope the product error, bound the signed numerator by the sum of absolute coefficients times panel endpoint powers, and integrate the approximant by exact power moments with 2048-bit outward real arithmetic. The standalone proof was written before its implementation.
- Result: eight calibration cases agree with independent elementary formulas: signed polynomials, a logarithm, a denominator of multiplicity 32, exponents -1/2 and 1/2, and two distinct scales. Widths range from about 4.49e-616 to 1.98e-37; runtime 0.169241 seconds. Exact input and all panel truncation orders and remainder bounds are retained.
- Artifacts: audit record multiscale_moments, proof, SHA-256 000889dd3e6da3f2c6d6be3711e7429a0fb63033b04fea43cf7bdb05c898e156; code/moments.py, SHA-256 ee1f99e70caedac315f196bfb9a8f6948a3f27202e38e709b993942416ca670a; code/check.py, SHA-256 c592970c764f6abea9eb4f8df94ba96230fcca39c4f7ad7d8b0d51a8761c1842; data/calibration.json, SHA-256 0354192ef2f5250db1403dc29614c0afeb92df22241dca53a1e2204a640b3268; verification/calibration.json, SHA-256 a2282e1d648bc7870aed9c483fac101a174b3a0418a35cac8385a0369c860748.
